- **sillok-add**: Capture a backlog issue — GH issue with Issue Type + self-assign + project status Backlog. No branch, no worktree, no milestone; pick it up later with /sillok-start &lt;N&gt;. Optional --parent N or owner/repo#N. ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-add.md))
- **sillok-design**: Brainstorm and write the spec for the current issue. Saves spec to &lt;SPEC_DIR&gt;/, pastes the full spec content into the issue body, sets project status to In Design AFTER user reviews and confirms the spec content. ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-design.md))
- **sillok-end**: Push branch, create PR per the sillok:end skill's templates, set project status to In QA on the active sub-issue, update parent legacy checkbox if any. Does NOT auto-merge. Done note embedded in PR body Summary section. ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-end.md))
- **sillok-epic**: Read a team PRD (md file / picker / Notion URL), validate it against the team PRD convention, and create a light Epic issue in epicRepo for cross-repo parenting. ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-epic.md))
- **sillok-execute**: Write the plan from the spec, dispatch subagent-driven execution, and run a final whole-branch review at the end. Per-task reviews are delegated to superpowers; only the end-of-plan review is mandatory. Sets project status to In Progress when plan saved. Auto-answers writing-plans handoff with subagent-driven (no re-prompt). ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-execute.md))
- **sillok-init**: Bootstrap a project for sillok. Detects repo, base branch, package manager, gitignored config files, and branch prefix automatically. Asks at most two questions (project URL when no board is detected; auto-detected area labels when candidates found), and nothing under auto-mode. Idempotent. ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-init.md))
- **sillok-prd**: Record a completed PRD markdown into epicRepo as &lt;domain&gt;/&lt;name&gt;/prd.md (record-only snapshot; Notion stays the source of truth). Outputs a commit-pinned permalink for /sillok-epic to link. ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-prd.md))
- **sillok-start**: Bootstrap a new feature — create GH issue with Issue Type + self-assign + project status Todo + linked branch. Pass a bare issue number (#N) to ADOPT an existing issue instead (full env setup, Backlog → Todo). Optional --parent N (same-repo) or owner/repo#N (cross-repo PRD epic). ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-start.md))
- **sillok-story**: Create or promote-to a story — always backed by a real integration branch and worktree. From a non-sillok branch creates a fresh story; from inside a sillok feature/bug/improvement/infra branch offers promotion of the current issue. A story is an in-repo composite (parent tracking issue + integration branch + worktree) typed `Story` on GitHub. ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-story.md))
- **sillok-sweep**: Housekeeping sweep of worktrees, issues, PRs and the board. Shows a cleanup plan for your approval before touching anything, then prints what still needs you, grouped by the action it is waiting on. Runs from any branch; no issue or worktree required. ([judeProground/sillok](https://github.com/judeProground/sillok/blob/HEAD/commands/sillok-sweep.md))

- **sim-android**: Boot the Android emulator (first AVD listed, or `$BYMAX_SIM_ANDROID`) and run the app from the current Expo project. Auto-detects whether `expo start` is enough (Metro reattach) or `expo run:android` is needed (rebuild + install + launch). Detects Android SDK at `$ANDROID_HOME` / `~/Library/Android/sdk`. If SDK or AVD is missing, prints exact install steps and stops. Honors `$APP_VARIANT`. Triggers, "rodar no android", "abrir emulador android", "sim android", "android emulator", "boot avd", "test on android", "iniciar emulador android", "/bymax-mobile:sim-android". ([bymaxone/bymax-agent-kit](https://github.com/bymaxone/bymax-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/codex/plugins/bymax-codex/references/upstream/bymax-mobile/commands/sim-android.md) · 2 copies)

- **singular-detect**: Detect a project's tech stack — languages, frameworks, databases, cloud and tooling — and compare it with installed singularskills skills to recommend skills to find, adapt or create. Use when the user asks which skills would help with their stack, or what skills are missing for the codebase they are working in. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/singular-detect.md) · ★4)
- **singular-find-skill**: Search skill marketplaces and GitHub for an existing skill on a topic, check that it fits the skill format, and install or adapt it. Use when the user needs help with a task none of their installed plugins covers, in any domain, and asks to find or install a skill for it. Not for writing a new skill; see /singular-create-skill. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/singular-find-skill.md) · ★4)
- **singular-improve-skill**: Review and rewrite an existing skill using the user's feedback, its recent output and current best practice, then summarize what changed. Use when a skill produced weak or outdated results and the user wants that skill revised, whether from a plugin or custom-made. Not for a brand-new skill; see /singular-create-skill. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/singular-improve-skill.md) · ★4)
- **singular-init**: Initialize singularskills in a project — create the workspace directory, choose which domains to activate and record the project configuration. Use when the user is starting to use singularskills in a new project or wants to change which domains are active in it. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/singular-init.md) · ★4)
- **singular-report**: Generate a written cross-domain report for one project or all of them — the decisions made per phase, open questions, approvals and skipped gates, how the domains line up and next steps — saved for sharing. Use when the user wants everything their workflows have produced so far summarized for themselves or stakeholders. Not for a quick progress table; see /singular-status. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/singular-report.md) · ★4)
- **singular-status**: Show every project across all workflows with each phase approved, written or missing, the last activity and any skipped or refused gates, as a table in the chat. - **sketch-wireframe**: Generate hand-drawn lo-fi sketch wireframes from MVP specs or an idea ([kumaran-is/claude-code-onboarding](https://github.com/kumaran-is/claude-code-onboarding/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/sketch-wireframe.md) · ★37)
- **sketchbook**: Explore directions, mood boards and page mocks before building, with Velloo's sketchbook ([velloo-design/velloo](https://github.com/velloo-design/velloo/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude/velloo/commands/sketchbook.md) · ★10)
